🔨 **2026-08-05 (evening) — F12 SLICE 2a FUNDED: the offline per-move EV-loss grader. No cluster compute.**
Joshua's call after the F13 close ("EV loss grader it is"). Spec **pre-registered before any result** → [EVLOSS_SPEC.md](measurement/analyzer_evloss_20260805/EVLOSS_SPEC.md) (`ece490c`); lever indexed + BACKLOG's wrong units sentence retracted (`daaad45`). What it does: replay an archived game, run the champion's search at **every ply of both seats**, report ΔQ = Q(best) − Q(played) with the **champion seat as the paired control** (which is also the acceptance gate — if the instrument scores the agent that generated the moves as materially lossy, it is mis-wired and no human number is reportable). ⚠️ **The design sketch's units claim was WRONG and is corrected in the spec (D1): pooled Q is dimensionless (`tanh(virtual_score/15)`), NOT points** — points need `≈15·atanh(Q)`, emitted only as a labelled estimate; the **exact tail (k≤2) is the one true points-scale grader** and is reported separately. Bucket thresholds are **measured** from a reseeded null (D2), because CL-070's floor is a rate, not a ΔQ threshold. Cost ~45 s/archive/pass — build delegated, no box booking. **Coach mode stays DEFERRED and gated** behind uncoached E4 volume + a `coached:` archive flag. 🧭 **2026-08-02 (early) — THE INVISIBLE BORDER IS FIXED ON THE PHONE (APP-ONLY). New games start at row 18 of 35, not row 6.** Joshua's approved rec ("I'll take your rec on recentering") = app-only, delivered on top of the 2026-08-01 flip build. **The bug:** the engine starts at (6, 15) with 6 rows of headroom above vs 28 below, and `StateUpdater.play_tile` bounds-checks `open_positions`, so a rule-legal cell above row 0 is **silently never offered** — 67.8% of games lose ≥1 placement, 2.6% of all placements, 100% above row 0. **The fix:** `Game` gains an opt-in `start_row`/`start_col` (the gap the Rust `GameConfig` already had and **Python could not express through `Game` at all**), refusing odd shifts and off-board rows exactly as `GameConfig::resolve` does; the bridge gains **`grid_rule`** — `"centered18"` (new app games) / `"engine6"` — carried in the save payload AND the E4 archive, **missing ⇒ engine6, unknown ⇒ raise**, with the Rust mirror driven from the same coordinates so the champion searches the grid on screen. ⚠️ **Shift = 12, EVEN on purpose** (banker's rounding in `offset_from_centroid_sums` is equivariant under even translations only ⇒ representation-neutral, bit-identical tensors). **MEASURED, not argued** — `scripts/diagnose_grid_wall.py` gained a `start_row` arg so the instrument that priced the bug prices the fix; 400 games/row, fresh seeds, same policy: row 6 = **64.8% of games denied / 2.33% of placements / 21,566 denials**, row 18 = **0.0% / 0.00% / ZERO, on any side**. ⚠️ **Still a RECENTRING, not a wall removal, but the caveat is ADVERSARIAL-ONLY** — the wall-seeking drive (always take the lowest-row placement) reaches row 0 at row 18 after ~60 tile plies; random play never gets there. ⛔ **THE GLOBAL/ENGINE DEFAULT IS UNCHANGED and its strict-xfail sentinel still XFAILS** — flipping *that* re-baselines everything and is **F9**, not this. **Verified on the Pixel** (`BridgeFlipDeviceTest` 4 → 5 legs, 5/5 green, raw `measurement/rustport_p7/device/flip_grid/`): retail start tile `city_top_straight_road` @**(18,15)**, 0 meeples, 71 left, lowest legal first row 18 · backend **rust**, note null, budget **11008** from the bundled YAML, `exp_fma true` / `tanh msun` · save carries **`grid_rule: centered18`** + `start_rule: retail` + `leaf_hash a36d2e15a3b3d71d` · **s/move 0.861/0.797/0.879** ⚠️ *opening plies, as before — does not supersede G7's 1.551 s ply-60 median* · **BOTH real E4 archives still replay**: 144/144 actions, scores `[111,113]` / `[73,108]` byte-equal, resolving to `engine6`/`engine`. ## Last verdict (2026-07-28 00:00) — **✅ THE SEARCH HAS NOT CONVERGED — but budget's effect on the move is SMALL, and the probe alone does NOT prove compression.** Budget shifts the deployed pick by **~4 points beyond reseed noise** (z 6.72), so "the search stopped changing its move" is refuted as the explanation for the flat curve. But **87% of the raw disagreement is the agent's own churn**, and it never measured move *quality* — so "the move changes but the change is nearly worthless" survives. **What implicates the ruler is CL-060, not the probe:** the same pair reads **+49.85 ± 17.55** head-to-head but **−21.4** through RoD-v2 — ~70 elo apart depending on who holds the ruler. ⇒ **Stop buying budget rungs graded against RoD-v2; priority returns to structural blocker #1.** Boxes idle, trees clean and synced; nothing promoted.

> ⚠️ **Corrected 2026-07-28 before commit.** The first write-up read "H1 refuted ⇒ H2 established". That is a false dichotomy — it was baked into the pre-registration and I carried it through. Joshua caught it: *if these disagreements are consequential, how does the 11008 agent barely beat the 2752 agent?* The answer is that they're only **weakly** consequential — ~4pp of move change buys ~+50 elo, thin for 4× the compute.

**🔬 MOVE-AGREEMENT PROBE — the answer to the question the curve below could not settle.** No opponent at all, so it sidesteps the elo-compression confound entirely. 873 positions drawn from the champion's own self-play at exactly the deploy budget, each replayed at 7 budgets × 3 independent seed lineages = **2694 records, 0 failed**. Pre-registered before any result existed, read-out code committed with it. Claim **CL-070**; pre-reg + full results → [MOVE_AGREEMENT_PREREG.md](measurement/classical_search/MOVE_AGREEMENT_PREREG.md); `results.csv move_agreement_k4_b28e9`.

| deploy 2752 → 4× 11008 | n | `D_cross` | null (reseed alone) | **Delta** | 95% CI | z | `D_paired` |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|---:|---:|
| **Overall** | 873 | 0.3039 | 0.2644 | **+0.0396** | [0.0283, 0.0517] | 6.72 | 0.2398 |
| **Narrow-gap** *(pre-registered headline stratum)* | 437 | 0.4622 | 0.4053 | **+0.0570** | [0.0374, 0.0776] | 5.57 | 0.3699 |

**Read `Delta`, never `D_paired`.** Reseeding alone at fixed budget already gives 26.4% disagreement; observed cross-budget is 30.4%. Budget's own share is **4.0 points = 13%**. H1 (`D_paired ≤ 0.05` **and** `Delta` CI covering 0) is refuted; `Delta`'s CI excludes 0 in *every* stratum, so H1-WEAK fails too.

⚠️ **Three things that must travel with this number.** (1) **The noise floor GROWS with depth** — `D_same` 0.1226 → 0.2997 across 344…11008. **At 11008 the agent disagrees with itself 30.0% of the time (44.9% narrow-gap)** at fixed budget — near-tie behaviour, and the reason 30% self-churn doesn't destabilise its strength. (2) **The validity guard passed narrowly in the headline stratum**: the pre-reg voids the probe at `D_same ≥ 0.5`, and narrow-gap `D_same(11008)` = **0.4493**. Quote it alongside the overall 0.2997, never the overall alone. (3) **Agreement is stability, not quality** — the probe cannot say the deeper pick is *better*, which is exactly why it cannot promote compression on its own. The elo figures above 2064 in the curve below are **floors of unknown slack, not measurements**.

**Successor experiment, no opponent needed:** score *both* picks — 2752's and 11008's — on the ~4pp of positions where they disagree, against the exact solver or a much deeper search. That turns "the move changed" into "the move improved" and prices budget directly.

**THE CURVE** — our champion playing **BLIND** (fair PIMC, curve125, exact-K≤2) vs **RoD-v2 iter_02 playing SIGHTED** (bare NeuralMCTS `fair_chance=False`, anchor knobs, its OWN v2.9+rs0.25 leaf, no tail; net on GPU via carc-orch). All 7 rungs **share band 70e9** ⇒ adjacent rungs are **deck-matched** (CL-046 CRN) — the deltas are sharp, **the absolutes are correlated and must not be quoted as independent**. n=200 paired (100 decks) each, 0 timeouts. Claim **CL-069**; `results.csv blindcurve_*`.

| ×deploy | total | alloc | winrate | wr z | elo | margin z |
|---|---:|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 0.125× | 344 | k4×86 | 0.458 | −1.20 | −29.6 | −0.73 |
| 0.25× | 688 | k4×172 | 0.465 | −0.99 | −24.4 | +0.25 |
| **0.5×** | **1376** | k4×344 | 0.603 | **+2.90** | **+72.2** | **+4.24** |
| 0.75× | 2064 | k4×516 | 0.635 | +3.82 | +96.2 | +6.05 |
| **1×** | 2752 | k4×688 | 0.675 | +4.95 | **+127.0** | +5.92 |
| 2× | 5504 | k4×1376 | 0.633 | +3.75 | +94.3 | +5.66 |
| 4× | 11008 | k8×1376 | 0.648 | +4.17 | +105.6 | +7.19 |

**1️⃣ THE TREND IS THE MEASUREMENT.** Within-deck slope, clean **k4-only** axis (344…5504): **+2.782 pts/deck per doubling (se 0.426, z +6.54)**; full frontier incl. the k8 rung +2.449 (z +7.26). Individual steps span z 0.16–2.90 and only one clears 2σ — **the line is the evidence**, the standard CL-054 used for a coherent monotone axis. (Joshua's point, and it was right.)

**2️⃣ RISES TO ~2064, THEN FLAT.** The *only* step clearing 2σ is **688→1376** (winrate +0.138 z +2.80 **and** margin +5.73 z +2.90 — both agree). Every step above 2752 is null on **both** statistics: 2752−2064 (wr z +1.00 / margin z −0.30) · 5504−2752 (wr z −0.92 / margin z −0.27) · 11008−5504 (wr z +0.39 / margin z +1.51). **This reproduces CL-068's flat top against an INDEPENDENT opponent** — much harder to dismiss as an artifact of grading the champion against itself. ⚠️ Note this *contradicts* the earlier cross-band screen that suggested halving costs ~50 elo out-of-lineage; on one band, deck-matched, 2752 vs 1376 is +2.46 pts/deck z +1.23 — real-signed but unresolved.

**3️⃣ WHAT IT DOES NOT SETTLE — and the probe now running.** Elo vs a **fixed** opponent compresses as the candidate climbs, so an unknown share of the flattening is **instrument, not agent**. Two live hypotheses: (a) **genuine convergence** — above ~2064 the search stops changing its move; (b) **compression** — the champion keeps improving and RoD2 (~h3200 tier) cannot register it. No game vs RoD2 separates them. **(a) is directly measurable with no opponent**: move agreement between budgets, against a **same-budget replicate noise floor** (the champion is stochastic — k determinizations per move — so a raw disagreement rate is uninterpretable without that floor). Pre-reg + launch in flight → `measurement/classical_search/MOVE_AGREEMENT_PREREG.md`.

⚠️ **Caveats that travel with the curve:** the 11008 rung changes **allocation** (k8, the measured optimum there) so the top step mixes budget with width — the k4-only subset is the clean axis. Fixed k4 **under-allocates** the low rungs (CL-068: k2 beat k4 at 688) so 344/688 are pessimistic. **Not cost-matched** (candidate/opponent ms/move runs 0.29× at the bottom to 8.5× at the top). **Transport:** RoD2's anchor rows on record were net-on-CPU; these served the same weights from GPU — cite as "same weights, GPU transport", never bit-identical.

**Ops incidents worth remembering.** (i) The 11008 rung first completed **short at n=160**: killing local for a W change left **40 stranded `.claim` files** that neither box retried, and the harness summarised over what it had. Cleared and topped up to full n=200/100 decks; partial kept as `summary_n160_PARTIAL.json`. (ii) Another session edited `fair_agent.py`/`mcts.py`/`eval_fair_puct.py` **mid-run**; local was relaunched from a **pinned worktree** (`/home/doctor/projects/carc-pinned-c72053a`) verified byte-identical to the laptop on all four agent sources + the harness md5. (iii) A failed cell **logged `rc=0`** — caught only because 8 s was implausibly fast. **✅ FIXED 2026-07-27 (`437f5a7`), and the original diagnosis was wrong:** `bare_net_opp_orch.sh` was never at fault (verified — it exits 1 correctly). The bug was in the **callers**, and it is a bash expansion trap — `echo "[$(date …)] rc=$?"` runs the command substitution *during word expansion*, resetting `$?` to 0 before the later `$?` is read, so **every** failed run logged rc=0. Found in **8 scripts** repo-wide (the classical-search queues, the W-bench, three clean-eval runners); all now capture `rc` on the next line, print `FAILED rc=N — the cell is INCOMPLETE`, and exit non-zero. Same family as (i): the eval path fails **open**. Worktrees also need `target/` and the Cython `.so` grafted in (both gitignored).


**⚠️ INTERPRETATION — NOT a clean "conservative lower bound" (corrected 2026-07-27; the harness banner now says the same — `eval_fair_puct.py` docstring, the summary/smoke/`[bare-net]` prints, `--opponent` help, and the manifest `asymmetry` block, which gained `is_bound: false` + a `favours` map).** The asymmetries do **not** all run our way: **information → we are HANDICAPPED** (blind vs sighted; measured clairvoyance tax ~156 elo, large) · **leaf → we are ADVANTAGED** (curve125 vs curve100+residual; CL-051 established curve125 is a real leaf-strength win) · **endgame → we are ADVANTAGED** (exact-K≤2 tail vs their bare none) · **cost → we SPEND MORE** (1745 vs 1266 ms/move = 1.38×, not cost-matched). Net direction **undetermined** ⇒ the licensed claim is the narrow one: *our champion, blind and at half budget, beats the RoD-v2 anchor agent playing sighted.* It does **NOT** license "the lineage gap is at least +105.6".


**Ops:** GPU serves **only the opponent** (our champion is classical, no net). One carc-orch server **per box, on its own GPU** (local RTX 5060 Ti, laptop RTX 4070 Laptop), per-host SHM names, `OMP_NUM_THREADS=1` on each **server's own** env line (unpinned it takes ~2574% CPU from the workers), `max_batch = W`, `78ch/12sc` peeked from the checkpoint rather than trusting carc-orch's defaults — a wrong plane count is a *silent* mis-encode, not a crash. ⚠️ **Transport caveat:** every RoD2 anchor row on record was net-on-**CPU**; these cells serve the same weights from the **GPU**. Measured divergence 0/140 chosen actions, but rule-of-three bounds that at ≤2.1%/move ⇒ cite as "same weights, GPU transport", never as bit-identical.

Then fp16/int8 quantisation, or a smaller distilled net. **NOT promoted; champion + `governance/PRODUCTION.yaml` UNTOUCHED.** CL-067 → **Supported/high** (strength confirmed, deployability refuted at current cost).

**🕐 NEW AND SCOPE-LEVEL — TOURNAMENT TIME CONTROL BLOCKS THE COMPUTE LEVER.** Delegated web research, fully sourced → **[docs/research/TOURNAMENT_TIMING_2026-07-26.md](docs/research/TOURNAMENT_TIMING_2026-07-26.md)**. Format confirmed to match our locked scope — 2-player, **base game only, Farmers included**. Move accounting resolved from our own records: a game logs `moves=144` over 72 tiles = **exactly 2.0 searched decisions per tile** (place tile, then meeple) ⇒ **~70 searched decisions per player**, so **~12.9 s available per decision**. |---|---:|---:|---:|---|
| **deploy champion k4×688** | 0 | **3.8 m** | **26%** | ✅ comfortable |

| 8× k16×1376 | +35.6 | 26.7 m | 178% | ⛔ loses on time |

**⇒ CL-060's ~+50 elo at 4× budget is REAL but CANNOT BE SPENT in a clocked game.** Compute is an **unclocked-play knob only**; any future citation of it as progress toward the superhuman goal must carry this constraint. The **deploy champion is the only comfortably legal config we have** — a point *in favour* of the current production setup. It also retroactively softens CL-067's failure: at 97% of clock the distilled net was never tournament-deployable either way. ⚠️ Caveats from the research: no-increment is inferred **from silence** in the WC rules, and UK/Polish controls could not be retrieved.

> **⛔ THE TWO BOLDED CLAIMS ABOVE ARE SUPERSEDED (stamped 2026-07-30, audit F9) — this table is SINGLE-STREAM pricing.** "Compute is an unclocked-play knob only" and "the deploy champion is the only comfortably legal config we have" were both true *of sequential execution*, which the table never says it assumes. **CL-071** (2026-07-29) makes the 4× config legal by **engineering, not by strength**: `parallel_workers=8`, measured **6.370×**, 30/30 action-identical roots ⇒ 11008 costs **~20.6%** of the 900 s clock (see CL-071 counterevidence (4) for the reconciliation — the widely-quoted 14.3–17.4% band divides the un-parallelized 34 s exact-K solve by the speedup). **k8×1376 was promoted and is the deploy champion.** Compute is now a *clocked-play* knob. The 8× (22016) row is **not** rescued by this: it is strictly dominated on strength anyway (+35.6 vs +49.9, CL-071), and its k-parallel cost was never benched.

**🆕 TWO LEVERS THIS OPENS (both now rows in [docs/LEVER_INDEX.md](docs/LEVER_INDEX.md), items 10–12 of the untried list):** (1) **PONDERING — search on the opponent's clock. NEVER-NAMED here, and it is explicitly LEGAL**: WC rules let a human pre-select their next tile during your turn, so top humans get **~2× our effective budget while our engine sits idle**. At 26% clock usage the champion has the headroom; pure engineering, no learned component. (2) **Sub-2752 budget points at PRODUCTION width** — the curve is measured at 1×/4×/8× but is **unmeasured BELOW deploy**; wanted for **clock margin**, not completeness (if ~1376 sims costs only ~−20 elo it halves clock usage to ~13%). The only low-budget ladder (CL-046 D0: +27.9/+61.4/+81.4/+149.3 at 800/1600/2752/5504) is `k_dets=8` and **k8→k4 alone is worth ~+66 elo at 2752** (CL-054), so its levels do not transfer — its shape (~−27 to −34 elo per halving) is indicative only.

**📕 PROGRAMME-LEVEL.** [docs/FLYWHEEL_POSTMORTEM_2026-07-24.md](docs/FLYWHEEL_POSTMORTEM_2026-07-24.md) — DRAFT for Joshua's review (evidence settled; the *framing* in §5/§6 is his call). Its §3 is the keeper: value *accuracy* saturates (~0.81, tile-draw luck) and accuracy is the wrong quantity — MCTS only asks *which sibling is better*, where an MSE head sits at chance (τ 0.08) vs the leaf's 0.58. **We are in the chess regime** (a computable evaluator exists), not the Go regime; NNUE is the existence proof and its price is search-derived targets at billions of positions, with the net placed *inside* classical search. ⚠️ §6.1 still frames the distill thread as merely "live" — superseded by the gate above. **Note the gate does NOT contradict the post-mortem**: it distils a *policy* and never asks a learned value to evaluate anything.

**🔧 NEW TOOLING / OPS FINDINGS.** [docs/LEVER_INDEX.md](docs/LEVER_INDEX.md) — 165+ rows keyed by *intervention* and every alias, with an explicit never-tried section (built after four killed levers read as "untried" on 2026-07-24; CLAUDE.md now says check it first and add rows). **⚠️ THE carc-orch STARVATION STORY WAS RE-MEASURED 2026-07-26 — the symptom was right, the diagnosis and the fix were both wrong.** Corrections: (1) **the gate never got the retune it was credited with** — `eval_champ.log` shows it ran start-to-finish at the DEFAULTS `fwd=2, max_batch=16`; no `fwd=6` run exists on disk, so the "~1.5–2×" was a projection, never a measurement. (2) **The box was not "87% idle" — it was SATURATED BY THE SERVER.** Measured at the gate's exact knobs (240 s warm-up, 3 samples; the control replicated an independent earlier probe to <1%):

| cell | workers agg | mean/worker | `carc-orch` | orch threads | GPU |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| control = what the gate ran | 367% | 12.2% | **2574%** | 66 | 37 W |
| `OMP_NUM_THREADS=1` on the SERVER | **932%** | 31.1% | **162%** | 36 | 52 W |

**The real defect: [fair_net_vs_net_orch.sh](scripts/classical_search/fair_net_vs_net_orch.sh) launches `carc-orch` with no thread limit and the Rust side never calls `set_num_threads`**, so libtorch sizes its intra-op pool to core count and OpenMP spins — 66 threads where the server's own design needs ~32 (W shm readers + forwarders + collector + watchdog). The wrapper pins the *Python client* to 1; that env never reached the server. Pinning it hands back ~24 cores, workers absorb them (**2.54× CPU utilisation**), and the **GPU does MORE work (37→52 W)** — which rules out "the freed CPU just went idle". **⚠️ CORRECTED SAME DAY AGAINST WALL-CLOCK: the 2.54× was a UTILISATION figure and OVERSTATED the real gain.** The n=400 confirmation ran in **~4.2 h** (two boxes, W48 + W26, pinned) vs the gate's **7.3 h** (one box, W28, unpinned) = **~1.7×** — and that is the pin *plus* W28→W48 *plus* an entire second box. Backing the laptop out (~15% of completions), the pin + W-change on one box bought roughly **1.4–1.5×**. Mechanism: aggregate worker CPU counts contended and stalled cycles as useful work, so freeing cores raises *utilisation* far more than *throughput*. **Quote ~1.5× for the pin, never 2.54×** — the pin is still free and worth having, just not worth what the proxy claimed. (Also: mid-flight `s/game` is biased *pessimistic* by startup amortisation — the mirror of the order-statistic trap; I revised this ETA down three times before it settled.) (3) **`max_batch` was the wrong knob to reach for anyway, and `fwd=6` unjustified.** On this path each request carries **k=1** (`make_remote_single_evaluator`) and the worker **blocks** on its response semaphore, so ≤W requests are ever outstanding while the collector batches on *positions* (`batcher.rs` `while total_k < max_batch`) ⇒ `max_batch < W` structurally forbids coalescing one round of workers into a single forward (the gate's mb=16 at W=28 = 57% coverage). The rule is **`max_batch ≥ W`**, i.e. mb is a *dependent* variable of W. And because workers block, extra forwarders have little to overlap — `fwd` should be probed at the winning W, not guessed. **⇒ CL-067's gate ran at ~40% of achievable worker CPU; its 7 h was substantially self-inflicted, and its 4.29× ms/move was measured while the net side paid this contention and the net-free champion paid none.** Still the second instance in two days of *a default carried across workloads* (the other: the laptop joiner missing `--shared-claim`) — and now also an instance of *an unverified fix being recorded as if measured*. **IN FLIGHT (2026-07-26 ~14:45, ~30 min):** per-box W ladders with `mb = W` and the server pinned — local W ∈ {16,24,32,40,48} (16C/32T), laptop W ∈ {12,16,20,24,32} (12C/24T; W has never been uniform across boxes) → `preflight_wladder_{local,laptop}/W_LADDER_RESULTS.tsv`. Throwaway band 99e9, `--no-results-csv`, nothing harvested.

**➡️ NEXT ACTIONS (nothing is running; both boxes idle):** (a) **pondering** — the highest-value new lever, legal, unexploited, no learned component, and it directly attacks the human's ~2× effective-budget advantage; needs scoping. (b) **sub-2752 cells at production width** — proposed pair: `k4×344` (1376) and `k2×688` (1376, width re-solved) vs the deploy champion, n=400 paired on a fresh band (**56e9 is now BURNED** by the CL-067 confirmation; 22/24/26/28/32/44/46/52e9 also burned — enumerate before picking). Cheapest cells on the curve (~1–1.5 h each on both boxes) and they also test whether width re-solving works *downward*. (c) **batching the k determinizations** into one eval request — the cheapest route to CL-067's needed ~4× cost cut, `MAX_K=8` already allows it. **Operating points are settled: local W48 / laptop W26** (reproduces the 2026-06-17 deployed config by a second instrument) and **pass `OMP_NUM_THREADS=1` to the carc-orch server** — it is NOT pinned by default and the server otherwise takes the box.

**B3 — DEAD, on the pre-registered statistic computed exactly as written.** The full 3-size × 2-seed ladder finished overnight (f256b8's two cells — the ones that had never completed an epoch in project history — ran under a cgroup cap at `MEM_MAX=16G`; min host free 11.8GB). Exact-solver ruler, max-k 2, **n=1119 roots, 0 errors**: per-size τ = **f64b4 0.1331 / f128b6 0.0953 / f256b8 0.0829** against the **v2.9 leaf at 0.6153**. Step deltas −0.0378 and −0.0124 → **slope (mean of the two) = −0.0251**. Gate: DEAD needs best τ < 0.25 **and** slope < +0.05 per ~4× params → **both satisfied**; LIVE (τ ≥ 0.3 on a rising curve) never approached (best single checkpoint of six = 0.1686). **Across a ~25× parameter range τ never moves toward the leaf ⇒ capacity is NOT the binding constraint**, extending M2/CL-039 to *"capacity- and data-limit-independent on this representation"*. Sanity guard PASSED (f64b4 replicate 0.1331 vs the required ~0.13, so this read-out is commensurate with the earlier §5A work). ⚠️ **Seed spreads (0.049–0.103) EXCEED the step deltas** — the monotone *decline* must not be read as real; the verdict rests on τ never approaching 0.25 (33% margin) and no size rising, both robust to that noise. → [CAPACITY_PROBE.md](measurement/capacity_probe/CAPACITY_PROBE.md) Results, `solver_score_capacity_full6.json`, `results.csv capacity_probe_solver_tau_ladder`, **CL-064**.

**🔓 CL-060 (throughput) RE-OPENED — it was closed on an UNDERPOWERED design, not a wrong one.** The n=400 paired H2H finished 01:31: candidate **k8×1376 (11008 sims) vs the deploy champion k4×688 (2752)** = **+49.85 ±17.55 elo (paired σ 14.32), W221/D15/L164, wr 0.5713, paired z 3.48, +2.98 pts/deck** — clearing its own **+35** re-open bar (trigger (a), written into CL-060 in advance). Registry status → **Reopened**.

**⚠️ A CORRECTION TO AN EARLIER READ IN THIS SAME BLOCK (2026-07-22, caught within the hour):** this was first written up as a *contradiction* — "the candidate is 12 elo WORSE vs the common h800 rung (+123.0 vs +135.0), a sign flip, ≈2.4σ". **That was the WRONG STATISTIC.** Those elo figures are unpaired summaries of W/L/D counts (σ≈18.5 each), but **all five ladder rungs share seed band 24e9, n=400, paired, with 0 deck_hash mismatches across all 400 games** — so the correct comparison is *paired*. Computed properly: **candidate MINUS champion, both vs h800, same 200 decks = +1.123 pts/deck, se 1.304, z=+0.86** — the candidate is **BETTER**, not worse, and there is **no sign flip**. (Champion vs h800 +8.643 pts/deck; candidate +9.765.) That `+1.12 / z0.86` is *precisely* CL-060's own recorded closure figure, i.e. the closure always rested on this paired statistic. **The two measurements agree in sign and are statistically consistent:** direct +2.98 (se 0.856) vs common-opponent +1.12 (se 1.304) → difference +1.86 ± 1.56, **z=1.19, n.s.** ⇒ **CL-060 was closed on an underpowered design, not refuted by a conflicting one.** The direct H2H is simply more sensitive: it drops the third party's variance and avoids **ceiling compression** from measuring two near-equals through an opponent both beat by ~9 pts/deck. Non-transitivity is NOT needed to explain anything.

**✅ THE CONFOUND IS RESOLVED — the decomposition landed 14:09.** The H2H moved **WIDTH and BUDGET together** (k8×1376 vs k4×688 at ~4× wall-clock), while CL-054 has k4 beating k8 *at fixed 2752* — so on its own it could not attribute the gain. | cell | what moves | elo | paired z | pts/deck |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| H2H (band 32e9) | width **and** budget | **+49.85** ±14.3 | 3.48 | +2.98 |
| decomp (band 44e9) | **budget only**, k=4 fixed | **+27.85** ±12.4 | **2.24** | +2.24 |
| ⇒ residual | width at fixed 11008 | +21.99 ±19.0 | 1.16 | +0.74 |

**Read: BUDGET converts and carries 56% of the gain, standing on its own; WIDTH is real-signed but NOT resolved.** ⚠️ The two cells sit on **different seed bands**, so the width residual is a difference of *independent* estimates added in quadrature — **not** a deck-paired contrast, hence the wide ±19. 💰 **The exchange rate is the actual finding: 4× compute → +27.85 elo ≈ +14 elo per DOUBLING of wall-clock ⇒ ~128× compute for +100 elo.** Compute is a **purchasable but expensive match-play knob**; it does **not** touch the structural blocker (learned components still don't exceed the hand-crafted leaf). **My recommendation: this does NOT by itself justify funding a throughput/search-core program.** ⚠️ **CL-054 tension, unresolved:** at 11008 the width sign is nominally *reversed* (+22 for k8) vs CL-054's k4>k8 at 2752 — consistent with "optimal width grows with budget", but z=1.16 does not establish it. ⚠️ **Honest caveats:** one unreplicated cell at nominal significance; +27.85 lands *between* the arms of the ≥+35 / ≈0 rule it was read against, and **that rule was mine, stated pre-result but NOT pre-registered** — so this is a measurement, not a gate outcome; ~+14/doubling is extrapolated from a single 4× step. **Sharp next tests, none funded:** (1) k8×1376 vs k4×2752 at fixed 11008 on ONE band (resolves width directly); (2) k4×5504 vs k4×1376 (second doubling — does the rate hold or bend?); (3) replicate the +27.85 on a fresh band. **Harvested: `results.csv {cl060_h2h_…, cl060_budget_…}`, CL-060 → Reopened, roadmap F5 re-stamped. > **⚠️ FIRST FAIR-TRAINED NET LINEAGE IN THE PROJECT.** We distill the **FAIR (blind PIMC) champion** (k4×688=2752, curve125), not the clairvoyant one — Joshua's call: clairvoyant distillation injects **strategy fusion** (the net is always blind, so it would learn `E_deck[π(board|true deck)]` = the average of deck-aware policies ≠ the optimal blind policy). Fair costs only **~1.3× clair** (measured; DESIGN's "4×" conflated the elo tax with compute). [PRIOR_GEN_MODE_AUDIT.md](measurement/distill_flywheel_20260715/PRIOR_GEN_MODE_AUDIT.md) verified **every** prior net (v1-v6, v25, deepteacher, residual attempt1/2, rod_v2/v28, M2) was clairvoyant-trained (`fair_chance=False` in `play_one_selfplay_game`) → the prior "learned can't beat the leaf" plateaus were all measured in the clairvoyant world, and none of them tested this. **NOTE the objective split:** policy head = **copy the teacher** (load-bearing, steers play); value head = trained on outcomes but **severed** (never consulted — search uses the frozen champion leaf). That deliberately sidesteps the value-vs-leaf ceiling that killed every prior attempt.

> **RESULTS — distillation WORKS; the bag does NOT.** Fidelity to the fair champion on a frozen 24-game probe (top-1 argmax match): **non-sighted (DONE)** 0.322 warmstart → 0.561 → 0.592 → **0.597**; **sighted (running)** 0.364 → 0.554 → 0.559. **(1) Distillation works:** 32%→~60% agreement = ~80% of the **~0.75 teacher-vs-teacher ceiling** (two strong searches only agree ~75%) and **2-2.6× any prior net's raw-prior/teacher agreement** ([PRIOR_TOP1_CALIBRATION.md](measurement/distill_flywheel_20260715/PRIOR_TOP1_CALIBRATION.md); the old clairvoyant champion iter8 retro-scores only ~0.37 vs the fair champion). **(2) The bag adds nothing** — sighted wins the *warmstart* (+4.2pp) then the edge evaporates once teacher signal arrives. **Correct claim = "no detectable gain", NOT "sighted is worse"** (noise floor ~3pp; iter-1 Δ=3.3pp ≈1σ — do not repeat the c=3 "+47" / deepteacher "+53.7" noise-spike errors). **History PREDICTED it** ([INPUT_EXPOSURE_HISTORY.md](measurement/distill_flywheel_20260715/INPUT_EXPOSURE_HISTORY.md)): CL-050's **bag-blind control recovered ~95%** of the offline gain (bag ≈5%); CL-037 ablation "largely redundant"; and **no input addition has EVER fired in games in this project** (the 78ch meeple-fix counterexample was never A/B'd). Ours is the first **policy** test + first with the value loop severed = the cleanest bag test yet, and it agrees. **STAY SIGHTED** (Joshua): no gain but ~zero cost, keeps bag optionality for the flywheel's *improvement* step.

> **⚠️ THE LADDER IS REJECTED AS THE REFERENCE + THE RULER TRAP.** The CL-046 fair ladder (champ vs h800: +27.9/+61.4/**+81.4**/+149.3 @ 800/1600/2752/5504) is **kd8 + curve100** = two config generations behind our k4+curve125 teacher. Its *rung* matches ours; its champion side is stale. **The rung being "weak" is NOT the flaw** — it's non-saturated (champion wr 0.615) = a fine ruler. → Measuring by **DIRECT head-to-head**, not delta-of-deltas through the rung (Joshua). ⚠️ **NEVER `source champ_env.sh` before `eval_fair_puct`** — its `_CANON_ENV` uses `os.environ.setdefault`, so a pre-set curve env WINS and silently moves `DEFAULT_CONFIG` **which IS the h800 rung** → shifts the CL-022 ruler and invalidates every cross-arm number while still looking plausible. curve125 is injected **in-process, candidate-side only**; `_assert_rung_is_ruler` fails loud. ⚠️ **Two leaf-hash dialects, same leaf:** `a36d2e15` (harness, meeple_k=2.0) vs `6dfffd57` (snapshot, meeple_k=0.0) — `meeple_k` is **inert** under a non-null curve (240/240 byte-identical); **PRODUCTION.yaml's `158f17ff` is STALE** (LeafConfig gained default-off fields) → assert curve VALUES, not a fingerprint. ⚠️ **carc-orch defaults n_ch=78** → a sighted net needs explicit `--n-ch 81 --n-scalar 42` or it silently corrupts.

> **BUILT (branch `rod_v2_flywheel`, not pushed):** `a1ae1db` fair emitter `gen_fair_distill.py` + `fair_agent` agg_n stash + stage-1 driver (21 tests) · `4a25a74` `--sighted` (default OFF = byte-unchanged) + sighted driver · **`04b951f` STAGE-2 FLYWHEEL** (`make_fair_net_prior_evaluator` + `--net-ckpt` orch mode + `run_distill_stage2.sh`; integration-smoked — orch serves sighted 81ch/42 end-to-end) · **`60be5b7` `--info fair-netprior` strength arm** (net policy priors + frozen curve125 leaf, both reps, rung guarded) · in flight: `--opponent {h800,fair-champion,net}` for direct head-to-heads. **NEXT:** stage-1 done ~1:15am → **two head-to-heads** (best distilled net **vs CHAMPION** = pure prior-swap at the same 2752 budget/leaf — note the net's priors encode the champion's *search output*, so they could **exceed** its raw one-ply priors, which is the flywheel's whole bet; and **sighted vs non-sighted iter_02** = does the bag matter for STRENGTH) → W28 orch bench → **flywheel launch (needs Joshua's go)**. **PRODUCTION.yaml / champion UNTOUCHED** — measurement/exploratory only.

Needs a small eval launcher (fair_agent has a smoke, not a full harness); K× cost. This is the number you'd put on a box, and the thing the whole deployment arc is missing.
2. **h12800 depth arbiter** — the ONE un-confirmed strength lever (h12800 vs h6400 screen **wr 0.605**, z2.27, n=100). Marginal (PRODUCTION already calls h12800 "marginally stronger"). ~3h; needs the xeon (UP, at `7237803` → **bundle-sync first**) or local. Confirm → deploy depth h6400→h12800.
3. **Fair-vs-clairvoyant at the champion's config** — the honest clairvoyance tax (measured only on iter8); one paired n=400, before any human-facing claim.
4. **Analyzer / Phase 5** — the **ORIGINAL win condition** ([docs/ORIGINAL_PROMPT.md](docs/ORIGINAL_PROMPT.md)), served by the proven **sighted value head** ("ranks positions but can't beat the leaf in search" is *exactly sufficient* for an analyzer). The natural pivot now the strength arc has converged.
5. **make/unmake endgame solver** — ~week eng; unlocks marginalized (fair) K3–4 exact endgame (fair mode can only solve K≤2 today). Deeper honest endgame. Empty worktree — needs a fresh start.
6. **Completeness / write-up:** ~2GB-subset capacity slope (crash-safe clean scale answer) · Gumbel search (the one canonical-AZ default never sampled — reviewer-proofing) · the **negative-result paper** (the learned-value closure + the F4-circularity / correlated-substrate methodology — beyond the academic prior; needs a related-work novelty sweep first).

**Boxes:** local stable (⚠️ no capacity/30GB-dataset jobs — they OOM-crash the VM); laptop DOWN (:2222 flap); xeon UP (bundle-sync before use). Rule: **one heavy job at a time; CPU-only evals W30.**

## (2026-06-30) — ✅ **STEP-2 PeNS CONCLUDED: genuine Gate-B (CL-038).** The learned value CAN rank (Step-1 Gate A passed: α>0, τ~0.43 at every search depth, +43% offline) but CANNOT drive the MCTS leaf better than v2.9 — weaning/adding it craters play across MSE, ranking, AND frozen objectives. Charter §10 "(a) passes, (b) fails" → **value can rank but can't drive search → ship the analyzer (endgame-2, the original Phase-5 goal), served by this sighted value head.** **SCOPED: this closes the learned-value-LEAF lever, NOT the superhuman goal — fresh approaches remain open.**

**The verdict — 3 nails** (full detail: [DECISIONS.md](DECISIONS.md) 2026-06-30 + CL-038; numbers: `experiments/results.csv` `step2_nail2_*`/`step2_frozen_*`):
1. **interior-τ HOLDS flat** across search depth (d1 0.42 / d2-3 0.45 / d4-7 0.43, n=4156/9126/1327) → not an F-B2 distribution mismatch.
2. **additive leaf** (heuristic at FULL weight + value added) craters 0.500→0.285 → it's the value ADDITION, not the heuristic subtraction.
3. **frozen value** (never retrained) craters 0.215 ≈ MSE 0.212 → not retraining-drift.
The 3 cratering arms @ blend 0.27 vs RoD2 iter02: MSE 0.212 / ranking 0.287 / frozen 0.215.

**Vetting that earned it (all 2026-06-30):** an external review caught the in-loop τ was depth-1-only (→ the interior-τ probe = nail 1). A 3-round code-review loop found+fixed **5 bugs** (`f008b88`+`de62cbe`: export-parity tol 1e-4→5e-4 [the frozen-run killer, now un-blocked], done-marker fabrication, gen orphan-sweep race, gen+eval orch pkill scoping) and **verified the crater results are NOT poisoned** (full n, intact pairing; result-critical leaf/POV/win-count paths correct). Additive leaf mode committed `c4be026` (opt-in, convex bit-identical).

**⚠️ Box health:** the local 5900XT had **5 unexpected/dirty shutdowns in 14 days** (Win Event 41/6008, no BSOD → power/thermal under sustained load) — memory `reference_local_box_dirty_reboots`. Long runs MUST checkpoint per-iter. **⚠️ 2026-07-21: a SECOND, distinct failure mode exists — don't conflate them.** A big-memmap job (B3's f128b6 cell) grows WSL guest page cache until vmmem starves Windows (`.wslconfig` grants 42GB of a 47.9GB host) → **Event 26 "Virtual Memory Minimum Too Low" → the WSL VM is torn down while the host survives** (no Event 41, `dmesg` empty). Confirmed at 07-21 13:55:35 **and 07-04 18:02:38** — so the "2026-07-05 dirty reboot" that B3's loss was blamed on **never happened**, and the naive retry killed the box again (f128b6_s0.log died at byte 437 both times). Check Event **41 vs 26** before blaming hardware; the fixes are opposite. Mitigation = `scripts/probe_5a/run_capped_cell.sh` (cgroup `MemoryMax` + Windows-free-RAM watchdog); memory `reference_wsl2_host_memory_teardown`.

Outcome (pre-move-controlled, NO collider): punish take vs miss +31pp even-score / +29pp vs-strong — **suggestive but AGENT-CONFOUNDED** (strong agents take+win; pre-move-margin control doesn't remove agent identity). **Verdict: the narrowing worked; RoD1 has a real but modest competitive punish-deficit; recommend ONE gated probe (distil h6400 tactical-punish / value-head recal) gated on ≥2σ winrate vs h6400_v2.8, else the 8pp wasn't the bottleneck. `MUST_BLOCK_CITY` needs a larger sample to quantify.** DIAGNOSTIC ONLY — no training on labels, nothing promoted, PRODUCTION.yaml + v2.7 UNCHANGED, v2.8 opt-in.

- **Results (FACT):** `block`/`avoid_feeding` = NOISE (random ≈ h6400, ~2–6pp spread, flat/negative outcome → KILL); `contest_merge` = coarse "searches-at-all" step (random 18 → all search ~45–49, saturated above greedy, outcome −11pp → descriptive); **`farm_claim` = the one credible motif** — discriminates (greedy 12% rule-decline → heuristics 60% → rod1 54% → random 24%) AND predicts winning (**+17pp** in close games, strength-confounded so suggestive-not-causal).
- **RoD1 deficit (FACT, clean, identical positions):** `farm_claim` 54% vs h6400 60% (−6pp overall) but **−16pp vs WEAK opponents (41 vs 57; EQUAL 53/53 vs strong)** and growing LATE (pre_endgame 24 vs 53). Deeper heuristic search h3200→h6400 buys ~0 extra behavior (all ~60%).

- **Headline (FACT, deck-paired both seats):** the exact tail's Δ-score-margin **SCALES ~linearly with depth** — vs h3200 **+0.65 (K=2,n400) → +1.26 (K=3,n400) → +1.94 (K=4,n94)**, z+4.5/+7.3/+2.8; vs RoD1 +0.65 → +1.42. **But the WINRATE does NOT follow:** deck-paired **0.526/0.537/0.568, NON-SIGNIFICANT at every depth** (z≤1.5; margin→winrate slope only ~1.6%/pt). A K=4 n=82 partial's 0.61/z2.09 was deck-selection bias → 0.568. results.csv `exact{2,3,4}_vs_{rod1,heur3200}_v28_*`.
- **Mechanism (FACT):** regret micro — h3200's OWN endgame degrades with depth (top-1 0.833 K=2 → **0.667 K=4 = tied with RoD1**), so exact's edge compounds. Part E — RoD1's leak is pure last-tile PLACEMENT (not meeple mgmt); h3200 shares 40% of those mistakes.
- **Verdict (INTERPRETATION):** exact endgame is PROVABLY better than the deep heuristic on margin but SUB-POINT and OUTCOME-NEUTRAL — sharpens the ruler, doesn't beat it. **Confined to the last ~2–4 tiles; blocker #2 (whole-game learned strength) stands.** Distillation EV low (value-calibration only). **Next: a non-saturated reference (h6400/h12800) + whole-game levers, NOT deeper endgame exactness.** MEASUREMENT ONLY — nothing promoted, PRODUCTION.yaml + champion (`flywheel2_champion_iter8`) + v2.7 UNCHANGED, v2.8 opt-in.
- **Loose ends:** K=4-vs-RoD1 only **n=12 (INCONCLUSIVE)** — interrupted by a local OOM (W=18 K=4 net-on-CPU blew 42GB; memory `reference_exact_solver_eval_infra`: K≥4 is **orch-incompatible** + net-CPU **RAM-bound**). Not re-run (low value — vs-h3200 carries the K=4 conclusion). **All 3 boxes killed/idle.** Branch `exact-endgame-hybrid`, commits `4675059`→HEAD (**unpushed**).

## Recent (2026-06-23) — Batch-512 calibration: COMPLETE ✅ — KEEP BATCH 256 (B512 ties B256 head-to-head but FAILS the parent gate + under-trains the policy; 1.31× speedup not worth it)
- **The question:** can batch size **512** replace **256** in the RoD v2.8 continuation recipe without weakening the net? Recipe-calibration only (1 sibling retrain, NOT a flywheel, NOT a promotion). Clean controlled experiment: trained `ROD_ITER1_B512_TEST` from the **same frozen iter8 parent** on the **same 1000-game v2.8 data** (dataset fp `61a12d76` IDENTICAL to B256, REUSED not re-gen), `--batch-size 512` the ONLY change (same lr/seed/epochs/leaf). Full report: **[measurement/rod_batch512_calibration/BATCH512_CALIBRATION_REPORT.md](measurement/rod_batch512_calibration/BATCH512_CALIBRATION_REPORT.md)**.
- **Speed (FACT):** B512 trains **1.31× faster** (58.2 vs 76.3 min, 3-epoch) but does **HALF the optimizer steps** (12,099 vs 24,196).
- **Training (FACT):** no collapse (entropy 1.5393), but the **POLICY head is under-fit** — val_pol plateaus at **0.435 vs B256's 0.270** (+61%), stable across epochs (the halved step count at fixed LR; not an epochs deficit). Value head unaffected (corr +0.4115).
- **vs FROZEN parent (FACT):** `B512+v2.8` vs `iter8+v2.8` = **+10.4 elo / paired z=1.65 — INCONCLUSIVE** (does NOT credibly beat the parent). On the SAME decks B256 was **+53.4/z3.51** — B512 keeps only ~1/5 of B256's parent margin. results.csv `b512_vs_iter8_v28_n400`.
- **vs B256 DIRECT (FACT, the decider):** `B512` vs `B256` (RoD_iter_01) head-to-head = **−1.7 elo / paired z=−0.41 — TIE** (point est −6.9 n200 → −1.7 n400). **The val_pol under-fit WASHED OUT under MCTS@200** (policy precision ≠ play strength). results.csv `b512_vs_b256_v28_n400`.
- **Non-transitivity (FACT):** transitivity predicted **−43** (from the +10.4/+53.4 parent margins) but direct is **−1.7** = ~41 elo gap (>2σ). B512 ties validated B256 yet lost B256's edge over the parent — corroborates "parent-relative Elo is a non-transitive proxy."
- **Root audit (FACT, 1000 v2.8 midgame pos):** B512 plays **0.737 like B256** (MORE than either vs parent 0.65/0.68) — a *less-converged version of the same policy*, not degraded; heur3200 agreement flat (Δ+0.010); no entropy collapse; divergence concentrated in the endgame.
- **Verdict (INTERPRETATION):** **KEEP BATCH 256.** B512 is strength-neutral head-to-head (under-fit laundered by search) but **fails the parent gate** (the defining RoD-positive result), under-trains the policy, and is **untested vs the heur ruler** where B256 reached parity (non-transitivity risk). The modest 1.31× speedup is dominated by a safe lever: **2-epoch batch-256 ≈ 1.5×** at zero risk. A properly LR-rescaled B512 is the only future-worth path (labeled variant, must clear the parent gate). MEASUREMENT ONLY: no promotion, **PRODUCTION.yaml + champion (`iter8`) UNCHANGED**, v2.7 frozen, v2.8 opt-in. `iter_01_b512.pt` sha `9cca3edf` (NOT champion). **Branch:** `rod_batch512_calibration`, commits `12558b6`→`ba766ea`.

Commits `dbdd4b3`→`c73b58a`.

## Recent (2026-06-22) — Heuristic v2.8 leaf branch: COMPLETE ✅ — meeple-economy term is a real, large CLASSICAL leaf gain; NOT an ML/superhuman lever (neural gate CLOSED) [the substrate RoD builds on]

NOT a promotion path until it exceeds the ruler. **Use batch 256** (the 2026-06-23 batch-512 calibration found 512 under-trains the policy + fails the parent gate — keep the validated recipe; if speed needed, 2-epoch batch-256 ≈ 1.5× at zero risk).
- Continue the measurement-first program: build a reference that isn't saturated above v2.7 (the gate on any superhuman claim). **Note (2026-06-22): RoD shows the learned agent can now REACH deep-heuristic strength at equal leaf — the binding gate is now an EXTERNAL/HUMAN anchor, since "parity with a hand-crafted leaf" is not superhuman.**
- **Optional:** complete the K=3 endgame suite (76 remaining positions) at low **W≤8** (the W=20 OOM lesson, banked in `feedback_worker_count_by_bottleneck`) for the full 150 — the partial already settles the qualitative finding.
- **Future tooling:** a make/unmake (no-deepcopy) endgame solver unlocks exact K=3–6.
